'template_contents'\n\nDATE_RE = re.compile(r'\\b(\\d{4})-(\\d{2})-(\\d{2})\\b')\n\n# Tags that represent layout caches we should remove from modified paragraphs.\n# We check by local-name substring match (case-insensitive).\nLAYOUT_CACHE_SUBSTRINGS = (\n    'lineseg',\n    'linebreak',\n)\n\n\ndef local_name(tag: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Return local-name for an XML tag, stripping namespace.\"\"\"\n    if tag is None:\n        return ''\n    if tag.startswith('{') and '}' in tag:\n        return tag.split('}', 1)[1]\n    return tag\n\n\ndef load_json() -> tuple[dict, list[str]]:\n    overview = json.loads(OVERVIEW_JSON.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))\n    actions = json.loads(ACTIONS_JSON.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))\n\n    summary = overview.get('summary', {})\n    if not isinstance(summary, dict):\n        raise ValueError('audit_overview.json: summary must be an object')\n\n    immediate = actions.get('immediate_actions', [])\n    if not isinstance(immediate, list):\n        raise ValueError('corrective_actions.json: immediate_actions must be an array')\n\n    immediate_strs = [str(x) for x in immediate]\n    return summary, immediate_strs\n\n\ndef build_placeholders(summary: dict, immediate_actions: list[str]) -> dict[str, str]:\n    \"\"\"Build mapping for {{placeholder}} substitution.\"\"\"\n    mapping: dict[str, str] = {}\n\n    for k, v in summary.items():\n        mapping[str(k)] = '' if v is None else str(v)\n\n    # Corrective actions placeholders\n    mapping['조치1'] = immediate_actions[0] if len(immediate_actions) > 0 else ''\n    mapping['조치2'] = immediate_actions[1] if len(immediate_actions) > 1 else ''\n    mapping['조치3'] = immediate_actions[2] if len(immediate_actions) > 2 else ''\n\n    return mapping\n\n\ndef rewrite_date_dashes_to_dots(text: str) -> str:\n    return DATE_RE.sub(lambda m: f\"{m.group(1)}.{m.group(2)}.{m.group(3)}\", text)\n\n\ndef apply_risk_severity_everywhere(text: str, risk_tier: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Replace every occurrence of risk tier with 'Value (Note)'.\n\n    Must apply to every occurrence of the risk tier string, not just placeholders.\n    Avoid double-appending if already has the note.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not risk_tier:\n        return text\n\n    note_map = {\n        'High': '즉시조치',\n        'Medium': '계획보완',\n        'Low': '모니터링',\n    }\n    note = note_map.get(risk_tier)\n    if not note:\n        return text\n\n    desired = f\"{risk_tier} ({note})\"\n\n    # If desired already present, do nothing.\n    if desired in text:\n        return text\n\n    # Replace standalone occurrences of risk_tier that are not already followed by a parenthesized note.\n    # Keep it conservative: word boundary match; risk tiers are ASCII words.\n    pattern = re.compile(rf\"\\b{re.escape(risk_tier)}\\b\")\n    return pattern.sub(desired, text)\n\n\ndef substitute_placeholders(text: str, mapping: dict[str, str]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Replace all {{key}} with mapping values.\"\"\"\n    if '{{' not in text:\n        return text\n\n    def repl(m: re.Match) -> str:\n        key = m.group(1)\n        return mapping.get(key, m.group(0))\n\n    return re.sub(r\"\\{\\{([^{}]+)\\}\\}\", repl, text)\n\n\ndef iter_xml_files(extract_dir: Path) -> list[Path]:\n    return sorted(p for p in extract_dir.rglob('*.xml') if p.is_file())\n\n\ndef build_parent_map(root: ET.Element) -> dict[ET.Element, ET.Element]:\n    parent: dict[ET.Element, ET.Element] = {}\n    for p in root.iter():\n        for c in list(p):\n            parent[c] = p\n matching elements anywhere under the paragraph.\n    parent_map = build_parent_map(paragraph)\n    to_remove: list[ET.Element] = []\n\n    for el in paragraph.iter():\n        ln = local_name(el.tag).lower()\n        if any(sub in ln for sub in LAYOUT_CACHE_SUBSTRINGS):\n            # Don't remove the paragraph itself.\n            if el is not paragraph:\n                to_remove.append(el)\n\n    for el in to_remove:\n        p = parent_map.get(el)\n        if p is not None:\n            try:\n                p.remove(el)\n            except ValueError:\n                # Already removed\n                pass\n\n\ndef process_xml_file(xml_path: Path, mapping: dict[str, str], risk_tier: str) -> tuple[bool, bool]:\n    \"\"\"Process one XML file. Returns (changed_anything, has_placeholders_after).\"\"\"\n    xml_bytes = xml_path.read_bytes()\n\n    # Parse preserving namespaces.\n    parser = ET.XMLParser(encoding='utf-8')\n    root = ET.fromstring(xml_bytes, parser=parser)\n\n    changed = False\n\n    # We'll mark paragraphs (<...:p>) whose descendants were modified.\n    modified_paragraphs: set[ET.Element] = set()\n\n    # Prebuild parent map for entire doc so we can walk up to paragraph nodes.\n    parent_map = build_parent_map(root)\n\n    def mark_paragraph(el: ET.Element) -> None:\n        cur = el\n        while cur is not None:\n            if local_name(cur.tag) == 'p':\n                modified_paragraphs.add(cur)\n                return\n            cur = parent_map.get(cur)\n\n    def transform_text(s: str, owner: ET.Element) -> str:\n        nonlocal changed\n        orig = s\n        s2 = substitute_placeholders(s, mapping)\n        s2 = rewrite_date_dashes_to_dots(s2)\n        s2 = apply_risk_severity_everywhere(s2, risk_tier)\n        if s2 != orig:\n            changed = True\n            mark_paragraph(owner)\n        return s2\n\n    for el in root.iter():\n        if el.text:\n            el.text = transform_text(el.text, el)\n        if el.tail:\n            el.tail = transform_text(el.tail, el)\n\n    #
